_source line 12_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I understand the data format:
- Each window in `windows_labeled.jsonl` has:
  - `labels`: dict with 3 LFs: `file_diff_delta`, `command_repetition`, `outcome_backpropagation`
  - Each LF votes: 1 (stagnation), -1 (progress), 0 (abstain)
  - `majority_label`: 1 (stagnation) or 0 (progress) — this is the ground truth
  - `confidence`: number of LFs that agree with majority

- Test set: `split_info.json` has `test_indices` — these are indices into the conf≥2 filtered array


- I need to look at the LF ablation results to understand the weights and how the confidence≥2 filtering affects the dataset.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have a clear picture:

Data format:
- Each window has `labels`: `file_diff_delta`, `command_repetition`, `outcome_backpropagation`
- LF values: 1 (stagnation), -1 (progress), 0 (abstain)
- `majority_label`: 1 (stagnation) or 0 (progress) — ground truth
- `confidence`: count of LFs that agree with majority

From LF holdout results, I can infer LF importance:
- Dropping file_diff_delta hurts most (crosseval macro_f1 drops from 0.8136 to 0.6329)


- Dropping outcome_backpropagation: crosseval 0.7535
- Dropping command_repetition: crosseval 0.7329

So file_diff_delta is clearly the most important, with the other two contributing more equally. I can derive weights from how much each LF damages performance when removed. There's an important caveat though: the test set is filtered to confidence ≥ 2, meaning the majority vote on those LFs defines the ground truth, so a majority vote baseline would achieve perfect accuracy on this filtered set.

Now I need to check how the test indices in the info file map back to the original filtered data, and then look at the existing code to understand the data loading pipeline.
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"LF Oracle Baseline — evaluate labeling function voting strategies without training.\\n\\nApplies various LF ensemble strategies on the same test split used by the GRU probe,\\nusing raw LF outputs from windows_labeled.jsonl (no embeddings, no training).\\n\\nInput:\\n    --data_dir: directory containing data/windows_labeled.jsonl\\n    --split_info: path to checkpoints/split_info.json (from train_probe.py)\\n\\nOutput:\\n    Markdown comparison table of LF baselines vs GRU probe (test_macro_f1=0.9055).\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nLF_NAMES = [\\\"file_diff_delta\\\", \\\"command_repetition\\\", \\\"outcome_backpropagation\\\"]\\nPROBE_REF_MACRO_F1 = 0.9055\\n\\n\\ndef load_windows(path: str) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load all windows from JSONL, preserving file order.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    windows = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if line:\\n                windows.append(json.loads(line))\\n    return windows\\n\\n\\ndef compute_prior(windows: list[dict]) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"P(progress) from training set majority labels.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    labels = [w[\\\"majority_label\\\"] for w in windows]\\n    return sum(labels) / len(labels) if labels else 0.5\\n\\n\\ndef predict_majority_vote(windows: list[dict], prior: float) -> list[int]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Use majority_label directly. All test windows should be non-abstain after filtering.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    fallback = 1 if prior >= 0.5 else 0\\n    return [w[\\\"majority_label\\\"] if w[\\\"majority_label\\\"] != -1 else fallback for w in windows]\\n\\n\\ndef predict_any_stagnation(windows: list[dict], prior: float) -> list[int]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Any LF votes stagnation(0) → predict stagnation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    fallback = 1 if prior >= 0.5 else 0\\n    preds = []\\n    for w in windows:\\n        vals = [w[\\\"labels\\\"][lf] for lf in LF_NAMES]\\n        if 0 in vals:\\n            preds.append(0)\\n        elif any(v != -1 for v in vals):\\n            preds.append(1)\\n        else:\\n            preds.append(fallback)\\n    return preds\\n\\n\\ndef predict_all_agreement(windows: list[dict], prior: float) -> list[int]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Predict only when all non-abstain LFs agree; otherwise use prior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    fallback = 1 if prior >= 0.5 else 0\\n    preds = []\\n    for w in windows:\\n        active = [w[\\\"labels\\\"][lf] for lf in LF_NAMES if w[\\\"labels\\\"][lf] != -1]\\n        if active and len(set(active)) == 1:\\n            preds.append(active[0])\\n        else:\\n            preds.append(fallback)\\n    return preds\\n\\n\\ndef predict_single_lf(windows: list[dict], lf_name: str, prior: float) -> list[int]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Single LF as classifier; abstain → prior.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    fallback = 1 if prior >= 0.5 else 0\\n    return [w[\\\"labels\\\"][lf_name] if w[\\\"labels\\\"][lf_name] != -1 else fallback for w in windows]\\n\\n\\ndef print_table(results: list[tuple[str, dict]]):\\n    header = \\\"| Method                      | Macro-F1 | Accuracy | Prec(M) | Rec(M) | F1(stag) | F1(prog) |\\\"\\n    sep = \\\"|\\\" + {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"# SWE-agent trajectories data loader for agent_progress_probe project.\\n# Loads and parses nebius/SWE-agent-trajectories JSONL data into structured objects.\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport json\\nimport re\\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nfrom typing import Optional\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass Step:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"A single step in an agent trajectory.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    index: int\\n    role: str  # \\\"system\\\", \\\"user\\\", or \\\"ai\\\"\\n    text: str\\n    mask: bool\\n    cutoff_date: Optional[str] = None\\n    system_prompt: Optional[str] = None\\n\\n    @property\\n    def is_action(self) -> bool:\\n        return self.role == \\\"ai\\\"\\n\\n    @property\\n    def is_observation(self) -> bool:\\n        return self.role == \\\"user\\\" and self.index > 0\\n\\n    @property\\n    def is_system(self) -> bool:\\n        return self.role == \\\"system\\\"\\n\\n    @property\\n    def command(self) -> Optional[str]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract the command from an AI step's code block. Returns None for non-AI steps.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if self.role != \\\"ai\\\":\\n            return None\\n        match = re.search(r\\\"```\\\\n?(.*?)```\\\", self.text, re.DOTALL)\\n        if match:\\n            return match.group(1).strip()\\n        return None\\n\\n    @property\\n    def has_file_display(self) -> bool:\\n        return \\\"[File:\\\" in self.text\\n\\n    @property\\n    def has_error(self) -> bool:\\n        return \\\"Traceback\\\" in self.text or \\\"Error\\\" in self.text\\n\\n    @property\\n    def has_test_output(self) -> bool:\\n        text = self.text\\n        return (\\\"PASSED\\\" in text or \\\"FAILED\\\" in text) and \\\"test\\\" in text.lower()\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass TestResult:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Parsed test results from eval_logs.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    passed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\\n    failed: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\\n    errored: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)\\n\\n    @property\\n    def total(self) -> int:\\n        return len(self.passed) + len(self.failed) + len(self.errored)\\n\\n    @property\\n    def pass_rate(self) -> float:\\n        if self.total == 0:\\n            return 0.0\\n        return len(self.passed) / self.total\\n\\n\\n@dataclass\\nclass Trajectory:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"A complete agent trajectory for one SWE-bench instance attempt.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    instance_id: str\\n    model_name: str\\n    target: bool  # True = resolved, False = unresolved\\n    steps: list[Step]\\n    exit_status: str\\n    generated_patch: Optional[str]\\n    eval_logs: Optional[str]\\n    row_index: int = 0  # source file line number, ensures unique trajectory_id\\n\\n    @property\\n    def num_steps(self) -> int:\\n        return len(self.steps)\\n\\n    @property\\n    def actions(self) -> list[Step]:\\n        return [s for s in self.steps if s.is_action]\\n\\n    @property\\n    def observations(self) -> list[Step]:\\n        return [s for s in self.steps if s.is_observation]\\n\\n    @property\\n    def issue_text(self) -> Optional[str]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"The issue description from step 1 (first user message).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if len(self.steps) > 1 and self.steps[1].role == \\\"user\\\":\\n            return self.steps[1].text\\n        return None\\n\\n    @property\\n    def has_patch(self) -> bool:\\n        return bool(self.generated_patch and self.generated_patch.strip())\\n\\n    def parse_eval_test_results(self) -> Optional[TestResult]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Parse test results from eval_logs.\\n\\n        Returns:\\n            TestResult with lists of passed/failed/errored test names,\\n            or None if eval_logs is empty or has no test section.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not self.eval_logs:\\n            return None\\n        start_marker = \\\"___TESTS_SECTION___\\\"\\n        end_marker = \\\"___TESTS_SECTION_END\\\"\\n        if start_marker not in self.eval_logs:\\n            return None\\n        start = self.eval_logs.index(start_marker)\\n        end = self.eval_logs.index(end_marker) if end_marker in self.eval_logs else len(self.eval_logs)\\n        section = self.eval_logs[start:end]\\n\\n        result = TestResult()\\n        for line in section.splitlines():\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if line.startswith(\\\"PASSED \\\"):\\n                result.passed.append(line[7:])\\n            elif line.s\\n\\n... into a Trajectory object.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        raw: A dict with keys: instance_id, model_name, target, trajectory,\\n             exit_status, generated_patch, eval_logs.\\n        row_index: Source file line number for unique identification.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        A Trajectory object with parsed Steps.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    steps = []\\n    for i, step_raw in enumerate(raw.get(\\\"trajectory\\\") or []):\\n        steps.append(Step(\\n            index=i,\\n            role=step_raw.get(\\\"role\\\", \\\"unknown\\\"),\\n            text=step_raw.get(\\\"text\\\") or \\\"\\\",\\n            mask=step_raw.get(\\\"mask\\\", False),\\n            cutoff_date=step_raw.get(\\\"cutoff_date\\\"),\\n            system_prompt=step_raw.get(\\\"system_prompt\\\"),\\n        ))\\n\\n    return Trajectory(\\n        instance_id=raw.get(\\\"instance_id\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n        model_name=raw.get(\\\"model_name\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n        target=raw.get(\\\"target\\\", False),\\n        steps=steps,\\n        exit_status=raw.get(\\\"exit_status\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n        generated_patch=raw.get(\\\"generated_patch\\\"),\\n        eval_logs=raw.get(\\\"eval_logs\\\"),\\n        row_index=row_index,\\n    )\\n\\n\\ndef extract_steps(trajectory: Trajectory) -> list[Step]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract the step sequence from a trajectory, excluding the system step.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        trajectory: A parsed Trajectory object.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        List of Steps starting from step 1 (the issue description),\\n        excluding the system prompt step.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    return [s for s in trajectory.steps if not s.is_system]\\n\\n\\ndef load_trajectories(path: str | Path) -> list[Trajectory]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load trajectories from a JSONL file.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        path: Path to a .jsonl file where each line is a JSON record.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        List of Trajectory objects.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    path = Path(path)\\n    trajectories = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for i, line in enumerate(f):\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            raw = json.loads(line)\\n            trajectories.append(parse_trajectory(raw, row_index=i))\\n    return trajectories\\n\\n\\ndef load_trajectories_streaming(path: str | Path, limit: int | None = None):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Lazily yield trajectories from a JSONL file.\\n\\n    Args:\\n        path: Path to a .jsonl file.\\n        limit: Maximum number of trajectories to yield. None for all.\\n\\n    Yields:\\n        Trajectory objects one at a time.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    path = Path(path)\\n    count = 0\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for i, line in enumerate(f):\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            raw = json.loads(line)\\n            yield parse_trajectory(raw, row_index=i)\\n            count += 1\\n            if limit and count >= limit:\\n                break\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    import sys\\n    data_path = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else (\\n        \\\"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0513/projects/\\\"\\n        \\\"agent_progress_probe/artifacts/data/swe_agent_trajectories_1000.jsonl\\\"\\n    )\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading from {data_path}...\\\")\\n    trajs = load_trajectories(data_path)\\n    print(f\\\"Loaded {len(trajs)} trajectories\\\")\\n\\n    resolved = [t for t in trajs if t.target]\\n    unresolved = [t for t in trajs if not t.target]\\n    print(f\\\"Resolved: {len(resolved)}, Unresolved: {len(unresolved)}\\\")\\n\\n    t = trajs[0]\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nSample trajectory: {t.instance_id}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Steps: {t.num_steps}, Actions: {len(t.actions)}, Observations: {len(t.observations)}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Has patch: {t.has_patch}, Target: {t.target}\\\")\\n\\n    test_result = t.parse_eval_test_results()\\n    if test_result:\\n        print(f\\\"  Eval tests: {len(test_result.passed)} passed, {len(test_result.failed)} failed, pass_rate={test_result.pass_rate:.2f}\\\")\\n\\n    edits = t.get_edit_commands()\\n    print(f\\\"  Edit commands: {len(edits)}\\\")\\n\\n    mid_tests = t.extract_mid_trajectory_test_results()\\n    print(f\\\"  Mid-trajectory test runs: {len(mid_tests)}\\\")\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.39}}"}

record.\\n\\n    Returns:\\n        List of Trajectory objects.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    path = Path(path)\\n    trajectories = []\\n    with open(path) as f:\\n        for i, line in enumerate(f):\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            raw = json.loads(line)\\n            trajectories.append(parse_trajectory(raw, row_index=i))\\n MajorityBaseline(BaselineModel):\\n    name = \\\"Majority\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        self.majority_class = 0\\n\\n    def fit(self, train_data, val_data=None):\\n        labels = [d[\\\"label\\\"] for d in train_data]\\n        self.majority_class = 1 if sum(labels) > len(labels) / 2 else 0\\n\\n    def predict(self, data):\\n        return [self.majority_class] * len(data)\\n\\n\\nclass CosineSimilarityBaseline(BaselineModel):\\n    name = \\\"CosineSim\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self):\\n        self.threshold = 0.5\\n        self.invert = False\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def _avg_adjacent_cosine(embeddings: torch.Tensor) -> float:\\n        if embeddings.shape[0] < 2:\\n            return 0.0\\n        e = torch.nn.functional.normalize(embeddings, p=2, dim=1)\\n        sims = (e[:-1] * e[1:]).sum(dim=1)\\n        return sims.mean().item()\\n\\n    def fit(self, train_data, val_data=None):\\n        tune_data = val_data if val_data else train_data\\n        labels = [d[\\\"label\\\"] for d in tune_data]\\n        scores = [self._avg_adjacent_cosine(d[\\\"embeddings\\\"]) for d in tune_data]\\n\\n        best_f1, best_t, best_inv = -1.0, 0.5, False\\n        for t in np.arange(-1.0, 1.01, 0.01):\\n            for inv in (False, True):\\n                preds = [int((s >= t) != inv) for s in scores]\\n                f = f1_score(labels, preds, average=\\\"macro\\\", zero_division=0)\\n                if f > best_f1:\\n                    best_f1, best_t, best_inv = f, t, inv\\n        self.threshold = best_t\\n Repeat3Baseline(BaselineModel):\\n    name = \\\"Repeat-3\\\"\\n\\n    @staticmethod\\n    def _predict_one(steps: list[dict]) -> int:\\n        ai_commands = []\\n        for s in steps:\\n            if s.get(\\\"role\\\") == \\\"ai\\\":\\n                cmd = extract_command(s.get(\\\"text\\\", \\\"\\\"))\\n                if cmd is not None:\\n                    ai_commands.append(cmd)\\n ### Assistant

_source line 50_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have all the info I need. Let me understand the pipeline:

1. `windows_labeled.jsonl` has 59496 windows
2. Filter: `majority_label != -1` → "usable" windows
3. Filter: `confidence >= 2` → "keep_indices" (indices into usable array)
4. Split: train/val/test indices (indices into filtered array)

The `keep_indices` are indices into the usable array (non-abstain), and the train/val/test indices are indices into the array formed by selecting `keep_indices` from usable.


The key insight is that on the conf≥2 test set, majority vote is the ground truth by definition since the labels were generated that way and high confidence means the vote is unambiguous. Now I'll write a comprehensive script that loads the data with the same filtering as the probe and evaluates on the conf≥2 test set, where majority vote should achieve perfect accuracy.

For the weighted vote approach, I'm calculating LF importance from the holdout crosseval results by looking at the performance drop when each LF is removed. File_diff_delta has the largest impact with a drop of 0.1807, followed by cmd_rep at 0.0807 and outcome_backprop at 0.0601, so I'll use these relative differences to weight the voting.

Writing the evaluation script...
```

Reports on both conf>=2 test set and conf=1 windows.\n\nEncoding: labels dict values: 0=stagnation, 1=progress, -1=abstain\n          majority_label: 0=stagnation, 1=progress\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport sys\nfrom collections import Counter\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, f1_score, precision_score, recall_score\n\nLF_NAMES = [\"file_diff_delta\", \"command_repetition\", \"outcome_backpropagation\"]\n\n# Weights derived from LF-holdout crosseval: delta in macro_f1 when each LF is removed\n# file_diff_delta removal: 0.8136 -> 0.6329 (delta=0.1807)\n# command_repetition removal: 0.8136 -> 0.7329 (delta=0.0807)\n# outcome_backpropagation removal: 0.8136 -> 0.7535 (delta=0.0601)\nLF_WEIGHT_DELTAS = {\n    \"file_diff_delta\": 0.1807,\n    \"command_repetition\": 0.0807,\n    \"outcome_backpropagation\": 0.0601,\n}\n_total = sum(LF_WEIGHT_DELTAS.values())\nLF_WEIGHTS = {k: v / _total for k, v in LF_WEIGHT_DELTAS.items()}\n\nPROBE_REF = {\"macro_f1\": 0.9055}\n\n\ndef compute_metrics(labels, preds):\n    return {\n        \"macro_f1\": round(f1_score(labels, preds, average=\"macro\", zero_division=0), 4),\n        \"accuracy\": round(accuracy_score(labels, preds), 4),\n        \"f1_stag\": round(f1_score(labels, preds, pos_label=0, zero_division=0), 4),\n        \"f1_prog\": round(f1_score(labels, preds, pos_label=1, zero_division=0), 4),\n    }\n\n\ndef mv_from_lf(w):\n    \"\"\"Recompute majority vote from raw LF votes.\"\"\"\n    active = [w[\"labels\"][lf] for lf in LF_NAMES if w[\"labels\"][lf] != -1]\n    if not active:\n        return -1  # all abstain\n    n_stag = sum(1 for v in active if v == 0)\n    n_prog = sum(1 for v in active if v == 1)\n    if n_stag > n_prog:\n        return 0\n    elif n_prog > n_stag:\n        return 1\n    else:\n        return 0  # tie-break to stagnation\n\n\ndef predict_majority_vote(windows):\n    return [mv_from_lf(w) for w in windows]\n\n\ndef predict_any_stagnation(windows):\n    \"\"\"Any LF votes stagnation → stagnation; else progress.\"\"\"\n    preds = []\n    for w in windows:\n        vals = [w[\"labels\"][lf] for lf in LF_NAMES if w[\"labels\"][lf] != -1]\n        if not vals:\n            preds.append(1)  # no info → progress (majority class)\n        elif 0 in vals:\n            preds.append(0)\n        else:\n            preds.append(1)\n    return preds\n\n\ndef predict_all_stagnation(windows):\n    \"\"\"ALL non-abstain LFs vote stagnation → stagnation; else progress.\"\"\"\n    preds = []\n    for w in windows:\n        vals = [w[\"labels\"][lf] for lf in LF_NAMES if w[\"labels\"][lf] != -1]\n        if not vals:\n            preds.append(1)\n        elif all(v == 0 for v in vals):\n            preds.append(0)\n        else:\n            preds.append(1)\n    return preds\n\n\ndef predict_weighted_vote(windows):\n    \"\"\"Weighted vote using LF importance weights from holdout ablation.\"\"\"\n    preds = []\n    for w in windows:\n        w_prog, w_total = 0.0, 0.0\n        for lf in LF_NAMES:\n            v = w[\"labels\"][lf]\n            if v != -1:\n                w_total += LF_WEIGHTS[lf]\n                if v == 1:\n                    w_prog += LF_WEIGHTS[lf]\n        if w_total == 0:\n            preds.append(1)\n        else:\n            preds.append(1 if w_prog / w_total > 0.5 else 0)\n    return preds\n\n\ndef predict_single_lf(windows, lf_name):\n    \"\"\"Single LF as classifier; abstain → progress (majority class).\"\"\"\n    return [w[\"labels\"][lf_name] if w[\"labels\"][lf_name] != -1 else 1 for w in windows]\n\n\ndef fmt_row(name, m):\n    return f\"| {name:30s} | {m['macro_f1']:.4f} | {m['accuracy']:.4f} | {m['f1_stag']:.4f} | {m['f1_prog']:.4f} |\"\n\n\ndef print_table(rows):\n    hdr = f\"| {'Method':30s} | {'M-F1':6s} | {'Acc':6s} | {'F1stag':6s} | {'F1prog':6s} |\"\n    sep = \"|\" + \"|\".join([\"-\" * 32] + [\"-\" * 8] * 4) + \"|\"\n    print(hdr)\n    print(sep)\n    for name, m in rows:\n        print(fmt_row(name, m))\n\n\ndef main():\n    base = Path(\"/root/autodl-tmp/agent_progress_probe\")\n    data_path = base / \"data\" / \"windows_labeled.jsonl\"\n    split_path = base / \"checkpoints\" / \"split_info.json\"\n\n    # Load all windows\n    all_windows = []\n    with open(data_path) as f:\n        for line in f:\n            line = line.strip()\n            if line:\n                all_windows.append(json.loads(line))\n\n    usable = [w for w in all_windows if w.get(\"majority_label\", -1) != -1]\n    print(f\"Total: {len(all_windows)}, Usable (non-abstain): {len(usable)}\")\n\n    with open(split_path) as f:\n        split = json.load(f)\n\n    keep_indices = split[\"keep_indices\"]\n    min_conf = split[\"min_confidence\"]\n    filtered = [usable[i] for i in keep_indices]\n    print(f\"Conf>={min_conf} filtered: {len(filtered)}\")\n\n    test_idx = split[\"test_indices\"]\n    test_windows = [filtered[i] for i in test_idx]\n    gt = [w[\"majority_label\"] for w in test_windows]\n    print(f\"Test set: {len(test_windows)}, distribution: {dict(Counter(gt))}\")\n\n    # Verify conf distribution in test\n    conf_dist = Counter(w[\"confidence\"] for w in test_windows)\n    print(f\"Test confidence distribution: {dict(conf_dist)}\")\n\n    # ========== SECTION 1: conf>=2 test set ==========\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"SECTION 1: LF Ensemble Baselines on conf>=2 Test Set\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print()\n    print(\"⚠ CAVEAT: Ground-truth labels are DEFINED by majority vote of LFs\")\n    print(\"  with conf>=2. Therefore, MV baseline achieves 100% by definition.\")\n    print(\"  This section shows LF ensemble behavior, NOT genuine predictive power.\")\n    print()\n\n    rows = []\n    rows.append((\"GRU Probe (probe_001)\", {\"macro_f1\": 0.9055, \"accuracy\": 0.0, \"f1_stag\": 0.0, \"f1_prog\": 0.0}))\n\n    strategies = [\n        (\"Majority Vote (=GT by def.)\", predict_majority_vote(test_windows)),\n        (\"Any-Stagnation\", predict_any_stagnation(test_windows)),\n        (\"All-Stagnation\", predict_all_stagnation(test_windows)),\n        (\"Weighted Vote\", predict_weighted_vote(test_windows)),\n    ]\n    for lf in LF_NAMES:\n        strategies.append((f\"Single: {lf}\", predict_single_lf(test_windows, lf)))\n\n    for name, preds in strategies:\n        rows.append((name, compute_metrics(gt, preds)))\n\n    print_table(rows)\n\n    # ========== SECTION 2: conf=1 (ambiguous) windows ==========\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"SECTION 2: Analysis on conf=1 (ambiguous) windows\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    print()\n\n    conf1_windows = [w for w in usable if w[\"confidence\"] == 1]\n    print(f\"Total conf=1 windows: {len(conf1_windows)}\")\n    conf1_gt = [w[\"majority_label\"] for w in conf1_windows]\n    print(f\"conf=1 label distribution: {dict(Counter(conf1_gt))}\")\n\n    print()\n    print(\"LF agreement patterns on conf=1 windows:\")\n    patterns = Counter()\n    for w in conf1_windows:\n        pat = tuple(w[\"labels\"][lf] for lf in LF_NAMES)\n        patterns[pat] += 1\n    for pat, cnt in patterns.most_common(15):\n        lf_strs = []\n        for lf, v in zip(LF_NAMES, pat):\n            sym = {0: \"S\", 1: \"P\", -1: \"_\"}[v]\n            lf_strs.append(f\"{lf[:3]}={sym}\")\n        print(f\"  ({', '.join(lf_strs)}): {cnt}\")\n\n    print()\n    print(\"LF strategies on conf=1 windows (ground truth = majority_label):\")\n    rows2 = []\n    strategies2 = [\n        (\"Majority Vote\", predict_majority_vote(conf1_windows)),\n        (\"Any-Stagnation\", predict_any_stagnation(conf1_windows)),\n        (\"All-Stagnation\", predict_all_stagnation(conf1_windows)),\n        (\"Weighted Vote\", predict_weighted_vote(conf1_windows)),\n    ]\n    for lf in LF_NAMES:\n        strategies2.append((f\"Single: {lf}\", predict_single_lf(conf1_windows, lf)))\n\n    for name, preds in strategies2:\n        rows2.append((name, compute_metrics(conf1_gt, preds)))\n    print_table(rows2)\n\n    # ========== SECTION 3: LF coverage stats ==========\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 70)\n    print(\"SECTION 3: LF Coverage on test set\")\n    print(\"=\" * 70)\n    for lf in LF_NAMES:\n        n_active = sum(1 for w in test_windows if w[\"labels\"][lf] != -1)\n        n_stag = sum(1 for w in test_windows if w[\"labels\"][lf] == 0)\n        n_prog = sum(1 for w in test_windows if w[\"labels\"][lf] == 1)\n        print(f\"  {lf:30s}: active={n_active}/{len(test_windows)} ({n_active/len(test_windows)*100:.1f}%), stag={n_stag}, prog={n_prog}\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nWeights used for weighted vote: {LF_WEIGHTS}\")\n\n    # Save results\n    output = {\n        \"conf_ge2_test\": {name: m for name, m in rows},\n        \"conf1_all\": {name: m for name, m in rows2},\n        \"lf_weights\": LF_WEIGHTS,\n        \"caveat\": \"conf>=2 test set labels are defined by MV, so MV baseline = ground truth (100%)\",\n    }\n    out_path = base / \"lf_ensemble_baseline_results.json\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF"
}

ABSTAIN\\n\\n\\ndef _cmd_similarity(a: str, b: str) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Character-level similarity between two commands using SequenceMatcher.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if a == b:\\n        return 1.0\\n    if not a or not b:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return SequenceMatcher(None, a, b).ratio()\\n\\n\\ndef _has_consecutive_similar(commands: list[str], min_run: int = 3, threshold: float = 0.9) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if there are ≥min_run consecutive commands with similarity > threshold.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(commands) < min_run:\\n        return False\\n    run = 1\\n    for i else:\\n            run = 1\\n    return False\\n\\n\\ndef lf_command_repetition(window: list[Step], trajectory: Trajectory, window_position: dict) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"LF2: Detect command repetition patterns as stagnation signal.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    commands = [s.command for s in window if s.is_action and s.command is not None]\\n\\n    if len(commands) < 2:\\n        return  failed += len(failed_lines)\\n    summary_passed = re.search(r\\\"(\\\\d+)\\\\s+passed\\\", text)\\n    summary_failed = re.search(r\\\"(\\\\d+)\\\\s+failed\\\", text)\\n    if summary_passed and passed == 0:\\n        passed = int(summary_passed.group(1))\\n    if summary_failed and failed == 0:\\n        failed = int(summary_failed.group(1))\\n    total = passed + failed\\n    if total == 0:\\n        return None\\n    return passed / total\\n\\n\\ndef lf_test_pass_rate_transition_deprecated(window: list[Step], trajectory: Trajectory, window_position: dict) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"LF2 (deprecated): Whether test pass rate is improving within the window.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    test_runs = []\\n    for step in window:\\n        if not step.is_observation:\\n            continue\\n        if not step.has_test_output:\\n            continue\\n        rate = _parse_test_pass_rate(step.text)\\n        if rate is not None:\\n            test_runs.append(rate)\\n    if len(test_runs) < 2:\\n        return ABSTAIN\\n    last = test_runs[-1]\\n    second_last = test_runs[-2]\\n    if last > second_last:\\n        return PROGRESS\\n    return STAGNATION\\n\\n\\ndef lf_outcome_backpropagation(window: list[Step], trajectory: Trajectory, window_position: dict) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"LF3: Label last windows based on final trajectory outcome.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    total_windows = window_position[\\\"total_windows\\\"]\\n    window_idx = window_position[\\\"window_idx\\\"]\\n\\n    is_last_two = window_idx >= total_windows - 2\\n\\n    exit = trajectory.exit_status\\n    if exit == \\\"exit_context\\\" or \\\"maximum iteration\\\" in exit or \\\"AgentStuckInLoop\\\" in exit or \\\"Timeout\\\" in exit:\\n        return STAGNATION\\n\\n    if not is_last_two:\\n        return ABSTAIN\\n\\n    if trajectory.target:\\n        return PROGRESS\\n    else:\\n        return STAGNATION\\n\\n\\nALL_LFS = [\\n    (\\\"file_diff_delta\\\", lf_file_diff_delta),\\n    (\\\"command_repetition\\\", lf_command_repetition),\\n    (\\\"outcome_backpropagation\\\", lf_outcome_backpropagation),\\n]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0},       "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Labeling functions for agent progress probe.\\n\\nEach LF takes a sliding window of steps and returns:\\n  1 = progress, 0 = stagnation, -1 = abstain\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport re\\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING\\n\\nif TYPE_CHECKING:\\n    from data_loader import Step, Trajectory\\n\\nPROGRESS = 1\\nSTAGNATION = 0\\nABSTAIN = -1\\n\\nEDIT_PATTERN = re.compile(r\\\"^edit\\\\s+\\\\d+\\\", re.MULTILINE)\\nSEARCH_CMDS = {\\\"search_dir\\\", \\\"search_file\\\", \\\"find_file\\\", \\\"find\\\", \\\"grep\\\", \\\"view\\\"}\\n\\n\\nOPENHANDS_EDIT_CMDS = {\\\"str_replace\\\", \\\"create\\\", \\\"insert\\\"}\\n\\n\\ndef _extract_edited_files(steps: list[Step]) -> tuple[int, set[str]]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Count edit commands and unique files edited in a window.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    edit_count = 0\\n    files = set()\\n    for step in steps:\\n        if not step.is_action:\\n            continue\\n        cmd = step.command\\n        if not cmd:\\n            continue\\n        cmd_stripped = cmd.strip()\\n        first_word = cmd_stripped.split()[0] if cmd_stripped else \\\"\\\"\\n        # SWE-agent: edit command\\n        if first_word == \\\"edit\\\":\\n            edit_count += 1\\n            idx = step.index\\n            for s in steps:\\n                if s.index == idx + 1 and s.is_observation:\\n                    match = re.search(r\\\"\\\\[File:\\\\s*([^\\\\]]+?)(?:\\\\s*\\\\(\\\\d+\\\\s*lines)\\\", s.text)\\n                    if match:\\n                        files.add(match.group(1).strip())\\n                    break\\n        # OpenHands: str_replace_editor tool calls\\n        elif first_word in OPENHANDS_EDIT_CMDS:\\n            edit_count += 1\\n            parts = cmd_stripped.split(None, 2)\\n            if len(parts) >= 2:\\n                path = parts[1].split(\\\"\\\\n\\\")[0].strip()\\n                if path:\\n                    files.add(path)\\n    return edit_count, files\\n\\n\\ndef _count_search_only(steps: list[Step]) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Count consecutive search/find commands without any edit.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    count = 0\\n    for step in steps:\\n        if not step.is_action:\\n            continue\\n        cmd = step.command\\n        if not cmd:\\n            continue\\n        first_word = cmd.strip().split()[0] if cmd.strip() else \\\"\\\"\\n        if first_word in SEARCH_CMDS:\\n            count += 1\\n    return count\\n\\n\\ndef lf_file_diff_delta(window: list[Step], trajectory: Trajectory, window_position: dict) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"LF1: Whether the agent is meaningfully modifying code in this window.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    edit_count, files = _extract_edited_files(window)\\n\\n    if edit_count >= 2 and len(files) >= 1:\\n        return PROGRESS\\n    if edit_count == 0:\\n        search_count = _count_search_only(window)\\n        if search_count >= 3:\\n            return STAGNATION\\n        return ABSTAIN\\n    return ABSTAIN\\n\\n\\ndef _cmd_similarity(a: str, b: str) -> float:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Character-level similarity between two commands using SequenceMatcher.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if a == b:\\n        return 1.0\\n    if not a or not b:\\n        return 0.0\\n    return SequenceMatcher(None, a, b).ratio()\\n\\n\\ndef _has_consecutive_similar(commands: list[str], min_run: int = 3, threshold: float = 0.9) -> bool:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Check if there are ≥min_run consecutive commands with similarity > threshold.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if len(commands) < min_run:\\n        return False\\n    run = 1\\n    for i in range(1, len(commands)):\\n        if _cmd_similarity(commands[i - 1], commands[i]) >= threshold:\\n            run += 1\\n            if run >= min_run:\\n                return True\\n        else:\\n            run = 1\\n    return False\\n\\n\\ndef lf_command_repetition(window: list[Step], trajectory: Trajectory, window_position: dict) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"LF2: Detect command repetition patterns as stagnation signal.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    commands = [s.command for s in window if s.is_action and s.command is not None]\\n\\n    if len(commands) < 2:\\n        return ABSTAIN\\n\\n    if _has_consecutive_similar(commands, min_run=3, threshold=1.0):\\n        return STAGNATION\\n\\n    if _has_consecutive_similar(commands, min_run=3, threshold=0.9):\\n        return STAGNATION\\n\\n    if len(set(commands)) == len(commands):\\n        return PROGRESS\\n\\n    return ABSTAIN\\n\\n\\n# --- Deprecated ---\\ndef _parse_test_pass_rate(text: str) -> float | None:\\n    passed = 0\\n    failed = 0\\n    passed_lines = re.findall(r\\\"PASSED\\\\s+\\\\S+\\\", text)\\n    failed_lines = re.findall(r\\\"FAILED\\\\s+\\\\S+\\\", text)\\n    passed += len(passed_lines)\\n    failed += len(failed_lines)\\n    summary_passed = re.search(r\\\"(\\\\d+)\\\\s+passed\\\", text)\\n    summary_failed = re.search(r\\\"(\\\\d+)\\\\s+failed\\\", text)\\n    if summary_passed and passed == 0:\\n        passed = int(summary_passed.group(1))\\n    if summary_failed and failed == 0:\\n        failed = int(summary_failed.group(1))\\n    total = passed + failed\\n    if total == 0:\\n        return None\\n    return passed / total\\n\\n\\ndef lf_test_pass_rate_transition_deprecated(window: list[Step], trajectory: Trajectory, window_position: dict) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"LF2 (deprecated): Whether test pass rate is improving within the window.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    test_runs = []\\n    for step in window:\\n        if not step.is_observation:\\n            continue\\n        if not step.has_test_output:\\n            continue\\n        rate = _parse_test_pass_rate(step.text)\\n        if rate is not None:\\n            test_runs.append(rate)\\n    if len(test_runs) < 2:\\n        return ABSTAIN\\n    last = test_runs[-1]\\n    second_last = test_runs[-2]\\n    if last > second_last:\\n        return PROGRESS\\n    return STAGNATION\\n\\n\\ndef lf_outcome_backpropagation(window: list[Step], trajectory: Trajectory, window_position: dict) -> int:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"LF3: Label last windows based on final trajectory outcome.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    total_windows = window_position[\\\"total_windows\\\"]\\n    window_idx = window_position[\\\"window_idx\\\"]\\n\\n    is_last_two = window_idx >= total_windows - 2\\n\\n    exit = trajectory.exit_status\\n    if exit == \\\"exit_context\\\" or \\\"maximum iteration\\\" in exit or \\\"AgentStuckInLoop\\\" in exit or \\\"Timeout\\\" in exit:\\n        return STAGNATION\\n\\n    if not is_last_two:\\n        return ABSTAIN\\n\\n    if trajectory.target:\\n        return PROGRESS\\n    else:\\n        return STAGNATION\\n\\n\\nALL_LFS = [\\n    (\\\"file_diff_delta\\\", lf_file_diff_delta),\\n    (\\\"command_repetition\\\", lf_command_repetition),\\n    (\\\"outcome_backpropagation\\\", lf_outcome_backpropagation),\\n]\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Sliding window extraction and labeling pipeline for agent_progress_probe.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nfrom __future__ import annotations\\n\\nimport json\\nimport sys\\nfrom collections import Counter\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))\\n\\nfrom data_loader import Step, Trajectory, load_trajectories_streaming\\nfrom labeling_functions import ALL_LFS, ABSTAIN\\n\\nWINDOW_SIZE_PAIRS = 5  # 5 action-observation pairs = 10 steps\\nSTRIDE_PAIRS = 2       # slide by 2 pairs = 4 steps\\nWINDOW_SIZE_STEPS = WINDOW_SIZE_PAIRS * 2  # 10\\nSTRIDE_STEPS = STRIDE_PAIRS * 2            # 4\\nMIN_STEPS_FOR_WINDOW = 12  # system + issue + at least 10 interaction steps\\n\\n\\ndef extract_windows(trajectory: Trajectory) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract sliding windows from a trajectory.\\n\\n    Skips step 0 (system) and step 1 (issue description).\\n    Returns list of window dicts with steps and metadata.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # Start from step 2 (first ai action after issue)\\n    interaction_steps = [s for s in trajectory.steps if s.index >= 2]\\n\\n    if not interaction_steps:\\n        return []\\n\\n    trajectory_id = f\\\"{trajectory.instance_id}__{trajectory.model_name}__r{trajectory.row_index}\\\"\\n    common = {\\n        \\\"trajectory_id\\\": trajectory_id,\\n        \\\"instance_id\\\": trajectory.instance_id,\\n        \\\"row_index\\\": trajectory.row_index,\\n    }\\n    windows = []\\n\\n    if len(interaction_steps) < WINDOW_SIZE_STEPS:\\n        # Short trajectory: single window with all available steps\\n        windows.append({\\n            **common,\\n            \\\"window_idx\\\": 0,\\n            \\\"steps\\\": interaction_steps,\\n            \\\"step_indices\\\": [s.index for s in interaction_steps],\\n            \\\"short_trajectory\\\": True,\\n        })\\n    else:\\n        idx = 0\\n        while idx + WINDOW_SIZE_STEPS <= len(interaction_steps):\\n            w_steps = interaction_steps[idx:idx + WINDOW_SIZE_STEPS]\\n            windows.append({\\n                **common,\\n                \\\"window_idx\\\": len(windows),\\n                \\\"steps\\\": w_steps,\\n                \\\"step_indices\\\": [s.index for s in w_steps],\\n                \\\"short_trajectory\\\": False,\\n            })\\n            idx += STRIDE_STEPS\\n\\n        # If there are remaining steps that form a partial window at the end,\\n        # include them only if we haven't already covered them\\n        if idx < len(interaction_steps) and idx != 0:\\n            remaining = interaction_steps[idx:]\\n            if len(remaining) >= 4:  # at least 2 pairs\\n                windows.append({\\n                    **common,\\n                    \\\"window_idx\\\": len(windows),\\n                    \\\"steps\\\": remaining,\\n                    \\\"step_indices\\\": [s.index for s in remaining],\\n                    \\\"short_trajectory\\\": False,\\n                })\\n\\n    return windows\\n\\n\\ndef label_windows(windows: list[dict], trajectory: Trajectory) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Apply all labeling functions to extracted windows.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    total_windows = len(windows)\\n\\n    for i, w in enumerate(windows):\\n        position = {\\n            \\\"window_idx\\\": i,\\n            \\\"total_windows\\\": total_windows,\\n            \\\"is_last\\\": i == total_windows - 1,\\n        }\\n        labels = {}\\n        for lf_name, lf_fn in ALL_LFS:\\n            labels[lf_name] = lf_fn(w[\\\"steps\\\"], trajectory, position)\\n\\n        w[\\\"labels\\\"] = labels\\n\\n        # Majority vote (excluding abstains)\\n        non_abstain = [v for v in labels.values() if v != ABSTAIN]\\n        if non_abstain:\\n            counts = Counter(non_abstain)\\n            w[\\\"majority_label\\\"] = counts.most_common(1)[0][0]\\n            w[\\\"confidence\\\"] = counts.most_common(1)[0][1]\\n        else:\\n            w[\\\"majority_label\\\"] = ABSTAIN\\n            w[\\\"confidence\\\"] = 0\\n\\n    return windows\\n\\n\\ndef process_trajectory(trajectory: Trajectory) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Full pipeline: extract windows and apply labels.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    windows = extract_windows(trajectory)\\n    if not windows:\\n        return []\\n    return label_windows(windows, trajectory)\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.32}}"}

Subgroup: 2 active LFs that disagree\n    print(\"\\n--- Subgroup: conf=1 with exactly 2 active LFs (disagreement cases) ---\")\n    conf1_2lf = [w for w in conf1\n        if sum(1 for lf in LF_NAMES if w[\"labels\"][lf] != -1) == 2]\n    if conf1_2lf:\n        c1_2_gt = [w[\"majority_label\"] for w in conf1_2lf]\n        n2_stag = sum(1 for g in c1_2_gt if g == 0)\n        n2_prog = sum(1 for g in c1_2_gt if g == 1)\n        print(f\"N={len(conf1_2lf)} (stag={n2_stag}, prog={n2_prog})\")\n        print()\n\n        patterns = Counter()\n        for w in conf1_2lf:\n            active_lfs = [(lf, w[\"labels\"][lf]) for lf in LF_NAMES if w[\"labels\"][lf] != -1]\n            key = tuple((lf[:3], \"S\" if v == 0 else \"P\") for lf, v in active_lfs)\n            patterns[key] += 1\n        for pat, cnt in patterns.most_common():\n            print(f\"  {pat}: {cnt}\")\n\n    # ===== SECTION 3: LF coverage on test set =====\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"SECTION 3: LF Coverage on conf>=2 test set\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    for lf in LF_NAMES:\n        n_act = sum(1 for w in test_windows if w[\"labels\"][lf] != -1)\n        n_s = sum(1 for w in test_windows if w[\"labels\"][lf] == 0)\n        n_p = sum(1 for w in test_windows if w[\"labels\"][lf] == 1)\n        print(f\"  {lf:30s}: {n_act}/{len(test_windows)} ({n_act/len(test_windows)*100:.1f}%) [S={n_s}, P={n_p}]\")\n\n    print(f\"\\nLF weights (from holdout delta): {LF_WEIGHTS}\")\n\n    # ===== SECTION 4: Key takeaway =====\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 80)\n    print(\"SECTION 4: Key Takeaway\")\n    print(\"=\" * 80)\n    print()\n    print(\"On conf>=2 test set, MV ≡ GT (1.0000) by construction.\")\n    print(\"Probe macro_f1 = 0.9055 < MV, but this is NOT a fair comparison.\")\n    print()\n    print(\"The probe's value lies in:\")\n    print(\"  1. Generalizing beyond MV-defined labels (learning from embeddings)\")\n    print(\"  2. Handling conf=1 ambiguous windows where LFs disagree\")\n    print(\"  3. Working on data where LF coverage is low\")\n    print()\n    best_single = max(\n        [(lf, compute_metrics(gt, predict_single(test_windows, lf))[\"macro_f1\"]) for lf in LF_NAMES],\n        key=lambda x: x[1]\n    )\n    print(f\"Best single LF on test: {best_single[0]} (macro_f1={best_single[1]:.4f})\")\n    print(f\"Any-Stagnation:         macro_f1={compute_metrics(gt, predict_any_stag(test_windows))['macro_f1']:.4f}\")\n    print(f\"GRU Probe:              macro_f1=0.9055\")\n    print()\n    print(\"The probe underperforms MV on conf>=2 (expected: MV=GT).\")\n    print(\"The meaningful gap: probe vs any-stag (0.9055 vs 0.8544) = +0.0511 on conf>=2.\")\n    print(\"On conf=1 windows, no probe evaluation exists yet (trained on conf>=2 only).\")\n\n    # Save JSON\n    output = {\n        \"conf_ge2_test\": {name: m for name, m in rows},\n        \"conf1_all\": {name: m for name, m in rows2},\n        \"lf_weights\": LF_WEIGHTS,\n        \"test_set_size\": len(test_windows),\n        \"conf1_size\": len(conf1),\n        \"notes\": [\n            \"conf>=2 test set: MV ≡ GT by construction → trivially 1.0\",\n            \"Weighted vote on conf=1 ≡ GT due to weight order matching Counter.most_common tiebreak order\",\n            \"Probe macro_f1=0.9055 vs best non-trivial LF baseline (Any-Stag)=0.8544 on conf>=2\",\n        ],\n    }\n    out_path = base / \"lf_ensemble_baseline_results.json\"\n    with open(out_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump(output, f, indent=2)\n    print(f\"\\nResults saved to {out_path}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nPYEOF"
}

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"#!/usr/bin/env python3\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"Evaluate probe on original test set (no baselines).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nimport argparse, json, sys\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\nimport torch\\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader\\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent))\\nfrom probe_model import ProgressProbe, WindowDataset, collate_fn\\nfrom sklearn.metrics import accuracy_score, f1_score\\n\\ndef main():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--checkpoint\\\", required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--data_dir\\\", required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--split_info\\\", required=True)\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--batch_size\\\", type=int, default=64)\\n    args = parser.parse_args()\\n\\n    device = \\\"cuda\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\"cpu\\\"\\n    data = torch.load(Path(args.data_dir) / \\\"embeddings.pt\\\", weights_only=False)\\n\\n    with open(args.split_info) as f:\\n        si = json.load(f)\\n    test_idx = si[\\\"test_indices\\\"]\\n    keep = si.get(\\\"keep_indices\\\")\\n    if keep is not None:\\n        data = {k: [data[k][i] for i in keep] if isinstance(data[k], list) else data[k]\\n                for k in data}\\n\\n    ckpt = torch.load(args.checkpoint, weights_only=False, map_location=device)\\n    model = ProgressProbe(\\n        embed_dim=ckpt[\\\"embed_dim\\\"],\\n        hidden_size=ckpt[\\\"hidden_size\\\"],\\n        num_layers=ckpt[\\\"num_layers\\\"],\\n    ).to(device)\\n    model.load_state_dict(ckpt[\\\"model_state_dict\\\"])\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    test_ds = WindowDataset(\\n        [data[\\\"embeddings\\\"][i] for i in test_idx],\\n        [data[\\\"labels\\\"][i] for i in test_idx],\\n    )\\n    loader = DataLoader(test_ds, batch_size=args.batch_size, collate_fn=collate_fn)\\n\\n    all_labels, all_preds = [], []\\n    with torch.no_grad():\\n        for padded, labels, lengths in loader:\\n            logits = model(padded.to(device), lengths)\\n            preds = (torch.sigmoid(logits) >= 0.5).long().cpu()\\n            all_labels.extend(labels.long().tolist())\\n            all_preds.extend(preds.tolist())\\n\\n    metrics = {\\n        \\\"macro_f1\\\": f1_score(all_labels, all_preds, average=\\\"macro\\\", zero_division=0),\\n        \\\"accuracy\\\": accuracy_score(all_labels, all_preds),\\n        \\\"f1_stagnation\\\": f1_score(all_labels, all_preds, pos_label=0, zero_division=0),\\n        \\\"f1_progress\\\": f1_score(all_labels, all_preds, pos_label=1, zero_division=0),\\n    }\\n    print(f\\\"Test windows: {len(test_idx)}\\\")\\n    for k, v in metrics.items():\\n        print(f\\\"  {k}: {v:.4f}\\\")\\n    # JSON output for parsing\\n    print(f\\\"METRICS_JSON={json.dumps(metrics)}\\\")\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": ## conf≥2 Test Set (N=1824)

| Method | M-F1 | Acc | F1stag | F1prog |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| GRU Probe (probe_001) | 0.9055 | 0.9282 | 0.8593 | 0.9518 |
| **Majority Vote (≡ GT)** | **1.0000** | 1.0000 | 1.0000 | 1.0000 |
| Any-Stagnation | 0.8544 | 0.8712 | 0.8050 | 0.9038 |
| All-Stagnation | 0.7659 | 0.8542 | 0.6222 | 0.9096 |
| Weighted Vote | 0.8154 | 0.8794 | 0.7067 | 0.9241 |
| Single: file_diff_delta | 0.7636 | 0.8531 | 0.6182 | 0.9090 |
| **Single: command_repetition** | **0.9451** | 0.9589 | 0.9175 | 0.9726 |
| Single: outcome_backpropagation | 0.7510 | 0.7955 | 0.6458 | 0.8563 |

## 关键发现

1. **MV ≡ GT**: conf≥2 test set 的标签就是 MV 生成的，所以 MV 达到 1.0 是恒等式，不是真正的预测能力

2. **command_repetition 单 LF (0.9451) > probe (0.9055)**: 因为 cmd_rep 覆盖率 97.4%，且在 conf≥2 test set 上它几乎总是属于多数投票方。这说明 probe 在 conf≥2 上没有超过最佳单 LF

3. **Probe 的真正价值在 conf=1 区间**: 39408 个 ambiguous 窗口（70% 只有 1 个活跃 LF，30% 是 2 个 LF 分歧），probe 目前未在该区间评估（仅在 conf≥2 上训练/测试）

4. **非平凡 baseline 最佳**: Any-Stagnation (0.8544)，probe 比它高 +0.0511
